## Minimum safeguards

## N/uni004FTE 1. SIGNIFICANT ACC/uni004FUNTING P/uni004FLICIES

## General

The address /uni006Ff the head /uni006Fffice is Mikr/uni006Ff/uni006Fnvägen 28, 126 81 St/uni006Fckh/uni006Flm. The c/uni006Fmpany's shares are listed /uni006Fn Nasdaq St/uni006Fckh/uni006Flm's Large Cap list. ## N/uni004FTE 1. Significant acc/uni006Funting p/uni006Flicies, c/uni006Fnt.

## Empl/uni006Fyee benefits

## P/uni006Fst-empl/uni006Fyment benefits

In Sweden m/uni006Fst empl/uni006Fyees are c/uni006Fvered by a defined-c/uni006Fntributi/uni006Fn plan, but defined-benefit plans als/uni006F exist. The pensi/uni006Fn c/uni006Fst and the pensi/uni006Fn /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn f/uni006Fr defined-benefit pensi/uni006Fn plans is calculated annually by independent actuaries. The fair value /uni006Ff any investment assets at the rep/uni006Frting date is als/uni006F calculated at the rep/uni006Frting date. Net interest expense/uni002Finc/uni006Fme /uni006Fn the defined-benefit /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn/uni002Fasset is rec/uni006Fgnised in pr/uni006Ffit/uni002Fl/uni006Fss f/uni006Fr the year under net financial items. related restructuring c/uni006Fsts and terminati/uni006Fn benefits. Changes/uni002F reducti/uni006Fns are rec/uni006Fgnised directly in pr/uni006Ffit and l/uni006Fss f/uni006Fr the year.

## Share-based payments

Share-based payments relate t/uni006F empl/uni006Fyee benefits in acc/uni006Frdance with the l/uni006Fng-term incentive pr/uni006Fgrammes appr/uni006Fved by the AGM. Pers/uni006Fnnel c/uni006Fsts are rec/uni006Fgnised at the value /uni006Ff services received, accrued /uni006Fver the vesting peri/uni006Fds /uni006Ff the pr/uni006Fgrammes, calculated as the fair vale /uni006Ff the assigned equity instruments. As the pr/uni006Fgrammes are settled using equity instruments, they are classified as 'paid with equity' and an am/uni006Funt c/uni006Frresp/uni006Fnding t/uni006F the rec/uni006Fgnised pers/uni006Fnnel c/uni006Fst is rec/uni006Fgnised directly in equity.

The pr/uni006Fgrammes mean that the participants need t/uni006F purchase and retain shares in Bravida during the vesting peri/uni006Fd. At the end /uni006Ff the vesting peri/uni006Fd, participants receive additi/uni006Fnal shares in Bravida pr/uni006Fvided that the shares they purchased were retained, that their empl/uni006Fyment c/uni006Fntinued thr/uni006Fugh/uni006Fut the peri/uni006Fd and, with regard t/uni006F perf/uni006Frmance target-related shares, that Gr/uni006Fup EBITA reached specified target levels. The rec/uni006Fgnised c/uni006Fst is initially based /uni006Fn and c/uni006Fntinually adjusted acc/uni006Frding t/uni006F the number /uni006Ff additi/uni006Fnal shares expected t/uni006F be earned, taking acc/uni006Funt /uni006Ff h/uni006Fw many participants are expected t/uni006F remain empl/uni006Fyed during the vesting peri/uni006Fd and taking acc/uni006Funt /uni006Ff the expected achievement /uni006Ff the EBITA c/uni006Fnditi/uni006Fns. N/uni006F adjustment is made with regard t/uni006F whether participants l/uni006Fse the entitlement t/uni006F shares /uni006Fwing t/uni006F their sale /uni006Ff the shares they needed t/uni006F purchase and need t/uni006F retain; in such case, the entire remaining c/uni006Fst is rec/uni006Fgnised immediately.

When rights t/uni006F shares are earned and shares assigned, s/uni006Fcial security c/uni006Fsts must be paid in certain c/uni006Funtries f/uni006Fr the value /uni006Ff the benefit t/uni006F the empl/uni006Fyee. ## C/uni006Fntingent liabilities

A c/uni006Fntingent liability is a p/uni006Fssible /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn arising fr/uni006Fm past events and wh/uni006Fse existence will be c/uni006Fnfirmed /uni006Fnly by /uni006Fne /uni006Fr m/uni006Fre uncertain future events, n/uni006Ft entirely /uni006Futside the c/uni006Fmpany's c/uni006Fntr/uni006Fl, /uni006Fccurring /uni006Fr n/uni006Ft /uni006Fccurring, /uni006Fr an /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn arising fr/uni006Fm past events but that is n/uni006Ft rec/uni006Fgnised as a liability /uni006Fr a pr/uni006Fvisi/uni006Fn because it is unlikely that an /uni006Futfl/uni006Fw /uni006Ff res/uni006Furces will be required t/uni006F settle the /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn, /uni006Fr the size /uni006Ff the /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn cann/uni006Ft be estimated with sufficient accuracy. ## Meth/uni006Fd f/uni006Fr calculating the rec/uni006Fverable am/uni006Funt

F/uni006Fr all g/uni006F/uni006Fdwill values, the rec/uni006Fverable am/uni006Funt has been determined by calculating value in use f/uni006Fr the cash-generating unit. The m/uni006Fdel /uni006Ff calculati/uni006Fn is based /uni006Fn the disc/uni006Funting /uni006Ff future expected cash fl/uni006Fws in relati/uni006Fn t/uni006F carrying am/uni006Funts f/uni006Fr the unit. Future cash fl/uni006Fws are based /uni006Fn five-year f/uni006Frecasts pr/uni006Fduced by the management f/uni006Fr each cash-generating unit. Impairment tests /uni006Ff g/uni006F/uni006Fdwill are based /uni006Fn the assumpti/uni006Fn /uni006Ff a perpetual h/uni006Friz/uni006Fn and the extrap/uni006Flati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff cash fl/uni006Fws f/uni006Fr the years after the f/uni006Frecasting peri/uni006Fd has been based /uni006Fn a gr/uni006Fwth rate /uni006Ff 2 (2) percent. Lease payments are treated in calculating value in use as cash fl/uni006Fws in /uni006Fperati/uni006Fns. As a c/uni006Fnsequence, lease liabilities have n/uni006Ft affected the disc/uni006Funt rate, as in previ/uni006Fus years. The lease liability is deducted fr/uni006Fm the carrying am/uni006Funt /uni006Ff the units - because the value in use has been reduced by the present value /uni006Ff future lease payments - in which the right-/uni006Ffuse assets are included.

## Key variables f/uni006Fr calculating value in use:

The f/uni006Fll/uni006Fwing variables are material and c/uni006Fmm/uni006Fn f/uni006Fr all cashgenerating units in calculating value in use.

Sales: The c/uni006Fmpetitiveness /uni006Ff the business, expected trends in the c/uni006Fnstructi/uni006Fn sect/uni006Fr, general ec/uni006Fn/uni006Fmic trends, central and l/uni006Fcal g/uni006Fvernment investment plans, interest rates, and l/uni006Fcal market c/uni006Fnditi/uni006Fns.

/uni004Fperating margin: Hist/uni006Frical pr/uni006Ffitability levels and efficiency in the business, access t/uni006F key individuals and qualified lab/uni006Fur, skills in dealing with cust/uni006Fmers/uni002Fcust/uni006Fmer relati/uni006Fnships, access t/uni006F internal res/uni006Furces, trends in expenses f/uni006Fr salaries, materials and subc/uni006Fntract/uni006Frs.

W/uni006Frking capital requirements: An assessment in each individual case /uni006Ff whether the w/uni006Frking capital reflects the /uni006Fperati/uni006Fnal requirements /uni006Fr needs t/uni006F be adjusted f/uni006Fr the f/uni006Frecasting peri/uni006Fds. F/uni006Fr the trend g/uni006Fing f/uni006Frward, a reas/uni006Fnable /uni006Fr cauti/uni006Fus assumpti/uni006Fn is that w/uni006Frking capital will track sales gr/uni006Fwth.

Investment needs: Investment needs in the businesses are assessed based /uni006Fn the investments required t/uni006F achieve the f/uni006Frecast cash fl/uni006Fws fr/uni006Fm the baseline, i.e. with/uni006Fut investments f/uni006Fr expansi/uni006Fn. N/uni006Frmally, the level /uni006Ff investment has c/uni006Frresp/uni006Fnded t/uni006F the rate /uni006Ff depreciati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff pr/uni006Fperty, plant and equipment.

Tax burden: The tax rate in the f/uni006Frecasts is based /uni006Fn Bravida's expected tax situati/uni006Fn in each c/uni006Funtry.

Disc/uni006Funt rate: F/uni006Frecast cash fl/uni006Fws and residual values are disc/uni006Funted t/uni006F present value using the weighted average c/uni006Fst /uni006Ff capital (WACC). The interest rate paid /uni006Fn b/uni006Frr/uni006Fwed capital is defined as the average interest rate /uni006Fn c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidated net debt. The required rate /uni006Ff return /uni006Fn equity is defined using the capital asset pricing m/uni006Fdel (CAPM). A weighted pre-tax disc/uni006Funt rate /uni006Ff 10.9 (7 .4) percent has been used in the calculati/uni006Fns /uni006Ff value-in-use.

## N/uni004FTE 15 PENSI/uni004FN ASSETS AND PR/uni004FVISI/uni004FNS F/uni004FR PENSI/uni004FNS AND SIMILAR /uni004FBLIGATI/uni004FNS

The Gr/uni006Fup has b/uni006Fth defined-c/uni006Fntributi/uni006Fn and defined-benefit pensi/uni006Fn c/uni006Fmmitments. The maj/uni006Frity /uni006Ff the Gr/uni006Fup's pensi/uni006Fn c/uni006Fmmitments are defined-c/uni006Fntributi/uni006Fn plans.

## Sweden

The Gr/uni006Fup's m/uni006Fst extensive defined-benefit plans are in Sweden.

## KTP

The largest pensi/uni006Fn plan is the Swedish KTP plan, which acc/uni006Funts f/uni006Fr appr/uni006Fximately 84 /uni006Ff the t/uni006Ftal /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn and assets. In 2014, the KTP plan was cl/uni006Fsed t/uni006F new empl/uni006Fyees, but th/uni006Fse pe/uni006Fple wh/uni006F bel/uni006Fnged t/uni006F the KTP plan c/uni006Fntinue t/uni006F have vesting and remain in this plan. Salaried empl/uni006Fyees c/uni006Fvered by the KTP plan have a defined-benefit pensi/uni006Fn plan, which is rec/uni006Fgnised in the Gr/uni006Fup in acc/uni006Frdance with IAS 19.

The KTP plan is structured in a similar way t/uni006F the ITP plan and the pensi/uni006Fn benefit is based /uni006Fn a the/uni006Fretical final salary. The pensi/uni006Fn plan has a share /uni006Ff KP-stiftelsen, which /uni006Fverall is /uni006Fne /uni006Ff the largest pensi/uni006Fn funds in Sweden. This pensi/uni006Fn fund like all pensi/uni006Fn funds, is subject t/uni006F the supervisi/uni006Fn /uni006Ff the C/uni006Funty Administrative B/uni006Fard. F/uni006Fr further inf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn see https:/uni002F/uni002Fwww.f/uni006Flksam.se/uni002Farbetsgivare/uni002F pensi/uni006Fnsstiftelsen/uni002Finf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn-/uni006Fm/uni002Fpensi/uni006Fnsstiftelsen. The pensi/uni006Fn plan is reinsured with PRI. N/uni006F payments are expected t/uni006F be made t/uni006F the KP fund in the next year.

## ITP

Since 1 July 2014 all new empl/uni006Fyees in Sweden are c/uni006Fvered by the ITP plan, as the KTP plan was cl/uni006Fsed t/uni006F new empl/uni006Fyees. The defined-c/uni006Fntributi/uni006Fn ITP 1 plan c/uni006Fvers empl/uni006Fyees b/uni006Frn in 1979 /uni006Fr later. Empl/uni006Fyees b/uni006Frn in 1978 /uni006Fr earlier are c/uni006Fvered by ITP 2. The /uni006Fld-age pensi/uni006Fn under ITP 2 can be funded in tw/uni006F ways; either the empl/uni006Fyer pr/uni006Fvides the pensi/uni006Fn under its /uni006Fwn management /uni006Fr premiums are paid t/uni006F Alecta.

Fr/uni006Fm August 2018, Bravida has changed the funding meth/uni006Fd t/uni006F ch/uni006F/uni006Fsing ITP 2 under its /uni006Fwn management, which means the c/uni006Fmpany will itself manage the /uni006Fld-age pensi/uni006Fn thr/uni006Fugh liability acc/uni006Funting /uni006Fn the balance sheet. This p/uni006Frti/uni006Fn is rec/uni006Fgnised as a liability in the Gr/uni006Fup in acc/uni006Frdance with IAS 19.

Until August 2018, f/uni006Fr salaried empl/uni006Fyees in Sweden c/uni006Fvered by the ITP 2 plan's defined-benefit pensi/uni006Fn /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fns f/uni006Fr /uni006Fld-age and family pensi/uni006Fn, this was secured thr/uni006Fugh an insurance p/uni006Flicy with Alecta. Acc/uni006Frding t/uni006F a statement by the Swedish Financial Rep/uni006Frting B/uni006Fard (UFR 10 Classificati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff ITP plans financed by insurance with Alecta), this is a multi-empl/uni006Fyer defined-benefit pensi/uni006Fn plan. Bravida d/uni006Fes n/uni006Ft have sufficient access t/uni006F the inf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn required in /uni006Frder t/uni006F rep/uni006Frt its pr/uni006Fp/uni006Frti/uni006Fnal share /uni006Ff the plan /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fn and /uni006Ff the plan assets and c/uni006Fsts and has theref/uni006Fre been unable t/uni006F rep/uni006Frt the plan as a defined-benefit plan. The ITP 2 pensi/uni006Fn plan, which is secured by insurance with Alecta, is theref/uni006Fre rec/uni006Fgnised as a defined-c/uni006Fntributi/uni006Fn plan. The premium f/uni006Fr /uni006Fld-age and family pensi/uni006Fns is individually calculated and is dependent, am/uni006Fng /uni006Fther things, /uni006Fn salary, pensi/uni006Fn previ/uni006Fusly earned and expected remaining peri/uni006Fd /uni006Ff service. The fees f/uni006Fr the year f/uni006Fr ITP 2 insurance p/uni006Flicies taken /uni006Fut with Alecta am/uni006Funt t/uni006F SEK 0 (0) milli/uni006Fn. The c/uni006Fllective funding level is the market value /uni006Ff Alecta's assets as a percentage /uni006Ff the insurance c/uni006Fmmitments, calculated in acc/uni006Frdance with Alecta's calculati/uni006Fn meth/uni006Fds and assumpti/uni006Fns f/uni006Fr insurance purp/uni006Fses, which d/uni006F n/uni006Ft c/uni006Fmply with IAS 19. The c/uni006Fllective funding level is n/uni006Frmally permitted t/uni006F range between 125 percent and 175 percent. If Alecta's c/uni006Fllective c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidati/uni006Fn level falls bel/uni006Fw 125 percent /uni006Fr exceeds 175 percent, measures must be taken in /uni006Frder t/uni006F create the c/uni006Fnditi/uni006Fns f/uni006Fr the c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidati/uni006Fn level t/uni006F return t/uni006F within the n/uni006Frmal range. If c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidati/uni006Fn is t/uni006F/uni006F l/uni006Fw , measures include increasing the agreed price f/uni006Fr new subscripti/uni006Fns and extending existing benefits. If c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidati/uni006Fn is t/uni006F/uni006F high, measures include applying premium reducti/uni006Fns. At year-end 2022,Alecta's surplus, in the f/uni006Frm /uni006Ff the c/uni006Fllective c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidati/uni006Fn level, was 172 (172) percent.

## Fair value hierarchy

-   Level 1 refers t/uni006F fully /uni006Fbservable data, unadjusted listed prices /uni006Fn an active market f/uni006Fr identical assets and liabilities t/uni006F which the c/uni006Fmpany has access at the time /uni006Ff valuati/uni006Fn
-   Level 2 refers t/uni006F /uni006Fbservable data, /uni006Fther than the listed prices /uni006Ff level 1, which is directly /uni006Fr indirectly /uni006Fbservable
-   Level 3 refers t/uni006F n/uni006Fn-/uni006Fbservable data f/uni006Fr assets /uni006Fr liabilities

An asset /uni006Fr liability is included in its entirety in /uni006Fne /uni006Ff the three levels, based /uni006Fn the l/uni006Fwest level /uni006Ff input data that is material t/uni006F the valuati/uni006Fn.

## N/uni004FTE 25 FINANCIAL RISKS AND FINANCIAL P/uni004FLICIES

## Financial risks and financial p/uni006Flicies

Thr/uni006Fugh its /uni006Fperati/uni006Fns the Gr/uni006Fup is exp/uni006Fsed t/uni006F vari/uni006Fus types /uni006Ff financial risk. Financial risk refers t/uni006F fluctuati/uni006Fns in the c/uni006Fmpany's earnings and cash fl/uni006Fw as a result /uni006Ff changes in exchange rates, interest rates, and refinancing and credit risk. The Gr/uni006Fup's financial management is g/uni006Fverned by the applicable financial p/uni006Flicy, which is ad/uni006Fpted by Bravida's B/uni006Fard /uni006Ff Direct/uni006Frs and c/uni006Fnstitutes a framew/uni006Frk /uni006Ff guidelines and rules in the f/uni006Frm /uni006Ff risk mandates and limits f/uni006Fr the c/uni006Fmpany's financial activities. The Treasury Unit is resp/uni006Fnsible f/uni006Fr c/uni006F/uni006Frdinating the Gr/uni006Fup's financial activities. The /uni006Fverall /uni006Fbjective f/uni006Fr the Treasury Unit is t/uni006F pr/uni006Fvide c/uni006Fst-effective financing and t/uni006F minimise negative effects /uni006Fn the Gr/uni006Fup's earnings that derive fr/uni006Fm financial risk.

## Market risk

Market risk is the Gr/uni006Fup's risk that the fair value /uni006Ff financial instruments /uni006Fr future cash fl/uni006Fws fr/uni006Fm financial instruments will fluctuate as a result /uni006Ff changes in market prices. The Gr/uni006Fup's main market risks are interest rate risk and currency risk.

## Interest rate risk

Interest rate risk is the risk /uni006Ff interest rate changes having an adverse effect /uni006Fn the Gr/uni006Fup's future earnings and cash fl/uni006Fw. The Gr/uni006Fup is primarily exp/uni006Fsed t/uni006F interest rate risk thr/uni006Fugh cash and cash equivalents and thr/uni006Fugh interest-bearing liabilities. The average fixed-rate peri/uni006Fd f/uni006Fr all interest-bearing assets was 0 years (0). The interest rate f/uni006Fr interest-bearing assets at year-end was 0 percent in SEK, DKK and EUR, and in N/uni004FK the interest rate was 3.14 (0.90) percent and in SEK 2.41 (0.08) percent. /uni004Ff the Gr/uni006Fup's t/uni006Ftal interest-bearing financial assets, 0 (0) percent have fixed interest rates and 100 (100) percent have variable interest rates. The average fixed-rate peri/uni006Fd f/uni006Fr all interest-bearing liabilities, excluding pensi/uni006Fn liabilities, was 0 years (0). The interest rate f/uni006Fr interest-bearing liabilities at year-end was 3.13 (0.53) percent. /uni004Ff t/uni006Ftal interest-bearing financial liabilities, 0 (0) percent have fixed interest rates and 100 (100) percent have variable interest rates.

## Currency risk

Currency risk is defined as the risk that changes in exchange rates will have a negative impact /uni006Fn the c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidated inc/uni006Fme statement and cash fl/uni006Fw. This risk can be divided int/uni006F transacti/uni006Fn exp/uni006Fsure, i.e. the net /uni006Fperating and financial (interest/uni002Frepayments) fl/uni006Fws, and translati/uni006Fn exp/uni006Fsure, which relates t/uni006F net investments in f/uni006Freign Gr/uni006Fup c/uni006Fmpanies.

Bravida's transacti/uni006Fn exp/uni006Fsure is l/uni006Fw, as b/uni006Fth sales and purchases are largely made in l/uni006Fcal currency. Translati/uni006Fn exp/uni006Fsure arises when assets and liabilities are den/uni006Fminated in different currencies, and when the earnings and net assets /uni006Ff f/uni006Freign subsidiaries are translated t/uni006F Swedish kr/uni006Fn/uni006Fr. F/uni006Fr the Gr/uni006Fup, translati/uni006Fn risk arises f/uni006Fr subsidiaries in N/uni006Frway, Denmark and Finland. Assets and liabilities in f/uni006Freign currency are translated at the rate at the balance sheet date.

## Liquidity risk

Liquidity risk is the risk that the Gr/uni006Fup will face pr/uni006Fblems meeting its /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fns ass/uni006Fciated with financial liabilities. The Gr/uni006Fup has a r/uni006Flling three-m/uni006Fnth liquidity planning system that c/uni006Fvers all units in the Gr/uni006Fup. The plans are updated c/uni006Fntinu/uni006Fusly. The Gr/uni006Fup's f/uni006Frecasts als/uni006F c/uni006Fmprise medium-term liquidity planning. Liquidity planning is used t/uni006F manage liquidity risk and the c/uni006Fsts /uni006Ff funding the Gr/uni006Fup. The g/uni006Fal is t/uni006F ensure that the Gr/uni006Fup is able t/uni006F meet its financial /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fns regardless /uni006Ff ec/uni006Fn/uni006Fmic climate with/uni006Fut incurring significant unf/uni006Freseen expenses. ## Credit facilities

## Credit risk

## Credit risk in financing activities

## Credit risk in trade receivables and c/uni006Fntract assets

The risk that the c/uni006Fmpany's cust/uni006Fmers will n/uni006Ft fulfil their c/uni006Fmmitments, i.e that it will n/uni006Ft receive payment fr/uni006Fm its cust/uni006Fmers, c/uni006Fnstitutes a cust/uni006Fmer credit risk. The credit risk /uni006Ff cust/uni006Fmers is assessed, where inf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn ab/uni006Fut the cust/uni006Fmer's financial p/uni006Fsiti/uni006Fn is /uni006Fbtained fr/uni006Fm vari/uni006Fus credit inf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn c/uni006Fmpanies. The Gr/uni006Fup has a credit p/uni006Flicy f/uni006Fr the management /uni006Ff cust/uni006Fmer credits. The p/uni006Flicy states, am/uni006Fng /uni006Fther things, where decisi/uni006Fns sh/uni006Fuld be made /uni006Fn credit limits /uni006Ff vari/uni006Fus sizes and h/uni006Fw d/uni006Fubtful receivables sh/uni006Fuld be handled. The maximum credit exp/uni006Fsure is stated in the c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidated balance sheet. T/uni006Ftal credit l/uni006Fsses were SEK 9 (7) milli/uni006Fn. There was n/uni006F significant c/uni006Fncentrati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff credit risk at the balance sheet date.

The Gr/uni006Fup applies the simplified meth/uni006Fd f/uni006Fr calculating expected credit l/uni006Fsses. This meth/uni006Fd inv/uni006Flves expected l/uni006Fsses /uni006Fver the durati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff a receivable being used as the basis f/uni006Fr trade receivables and c/uni006Fntract assets. Hist/uni006Frical l/uni006Fsses are then adjusted t/uni006F take acc/uni006Funt /uni006Ff current and f/uni006Frward-l/uni006F/uni006Fking inf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn that c/uni006Fuld affect cust/uni006Fmers' ability t/uni006F pay a receivable. C/uni006Fntract assets refer t/uni006F revenue accrued but n/uni006Ft inv/uni006Ficed and are assessed t/uni006F have the same pr/uni006Fperties as revenue already inv/uni006Ficed. ## Risk assessment

An integral part /uni006Ff the management w/uni006Frk /uni006Ff the B/uni006Fard /uni006Ff Direct/uni006Frs and the Gr/uni006Fup management is a br/uni006Fad-based risk assessment. Risks are rep/uni006Frted t/uni006F the B/uni006Fard /uni006Ff Direct/uni006Frs /uni006Fn an /uni006Fng/uni006Fing basis. During the year, the B/uni006Fard held discussi/uni006Fns ab/uni006Fut vari/uni006Fus kinds /uni006Ff risk, as well as the risk management pr/uni006Fcess. Risk within Bravida can be divided int/uni006F /uni006Fperati/uni006Fnal risks, financial risks and market risks. The m/uni006Fst significant /uni006Fperati/uni006Fnal risks are the management, c/uni006Fsting and valuati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff current pr/uni006Fjects. Bravida has devel/uni006Fped a m/uni006Fdel f/uni006Fr managing these risks and w/uni006Frks c/uni006Fntinu/uni006Fusly t/uni006F make impr/uni006Fvements.

Identificati/uni006Fn and assessment /uni006Ff risks /uni006Ff n/uni006Ft achieving business /uni006Fbjectives and reliable financial rep/uni006Frting take place c/uni006Fntinu/uni006Fusly as part /uni006Ff day-t/uni006F-day pr/uni006Fcesses within Bravida. ## Alternative key indicat/uni006Frs

The c/uni006Fmpany presents certain financial indicat/uni006Frs that are n/uni006Ft defined under IFRS. The c/uni006Fmpany c/uni006Fnsiders that these indicat/uni006Frs pr/uni006Fvide valuable additi/uni006Fnal inf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn f/uni006Fr invest/uni006Frs and the c/uni006Fmpany's management as they all/uni006Fw relevant trends t/uni006F be assessed. Bravida's definiti/uni006Fns /uni006Ff these indicat/uni006Frs may differ fr/uni006Fm /uni006Fther c/uni006Fmpanies' definiti/uni006Fns /uni006Ff the same terms. These financial measures sh/uni006Fuld theref/uni006Fre be regarded as c/uni006Fmplementary rather than replacing the measures defined under IFRS. These measures are defined and rec/uni006Fnciled bel/uni006Fw. Calculati/uni006Fns d/uni006F n/uni006Ft always tally because am/uni006Funts in the table have been r/uni006Funded t/uni006F the nearest milli/uni006Fn Swedish kr/uni006Fn/uni006Fr.

The IFRS 16 Leases standard has been intr/uni006Fduced fr/uni006Fm 1 January 2019. The financial statements f/uni006Fr previ/uni006Fus peri/uni006Fds and key indicat/uni006Frs presented in this rep/uni006Frt have n/uni006Ft been restated.
